...RESPONDENTS
Petition under Article 226 ot the Constitution of lndia_ praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one ih
the nature of Writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of .the respondent
authorilies in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than Ac.
2400.00 gts., in Sy.No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar Mandal,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee W/o. Burhanuddin, and in
view of the land made into small plots to an extent of 10O Sq. Yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt. Haneefa Bee
viz., Md. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dl. 0410412023,
foll,owed by personal visits, as being illega!, ar$itrary, uniust unconstit+tir:+al and
violative of Articles 21 and 300-4 of the Constitution of lndia and consequently
direct the respondent authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the
vacant, peaceful and physical possession of the plots to the petitioners as per
their Memorandum of Gift.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under Section '! 51 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated
in the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High Court may be pleased to
direct the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant

d

Petition under Article 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased toto issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of Writ of lvlandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than Ac.
2400.00 gts., in Sy.No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar Mandal,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the Government as
care taker by the originat pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee wlo. Burhanuddin; and in

4^
view of the land made inlo small plots to an extent of 100 Sq. Yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt, Haneefa Bee
viz., Md- Yousut Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dl. 2510312023 followed
by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjust unconstitutional and violative of
erticles 21 and 300-A oathe bonstitution of lndia and consequently direct the
respondent authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the vacant'
peacefulandphysicalpossessionoflheplotstothepetitionersaSpertheir
Memorandum of Gift.
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High court may be pleased to direct
the respondent authoiiiies to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant to the
representation dl.25to3l2o23, pending disposal of the main writ Petition, in the

-Constitution of India and consequently direct the

H1
Petition under Nlicle 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ,. Order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of Writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than Ac.
2400.00 gts., in Sy.No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar Handal,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Custocly of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee Wo. Burhanuddin, and in
view of the land made into small plots to an extent of 100 Sq. Yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt. Haneefa Bee
viz., Md. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dl. 1110412023 followed
by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, un,ust. unconstitutional and violative
of Articles 21 and 300-A of the Constitution of lndia and consequently direct the
respondent authorities to release the enlire land and lo deliver the vacant, pecefui
and physicalgossession of the plots to the petitioners as per their Memorandum of
Gift.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances slated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High Court may be pleased to direct
the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant to the
representalion dt. 11-04-2023, pending disposal of the main Writ Petition, in the
interest of justice.

..,RESPONDENTS
Petition under Nlicle 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one in
lhe nature of Writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than Ac.
2400.00 gts., in Sy.No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar Mandal,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee W/o. Burhanuddin, and in
view of the land made into small plots to an extent of 100 Sq. Yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt. Haneefa Bee
viz., Md. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dt. 29-03-2023 followed
by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjusl unconstitutional and violative of
Articles 21 and 3O0-A of the Constittuion of lndia and consequently direct the

/'\
respondent authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the vacant,
peacefulandphysicalpossessionoftheplotstothepetitionersaspertheir
Memorandum of Gift.
IA NO:1 OF 2024
W.P.NO :561 OF 2024
Petition under Section 151 cPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High court may be pleased to direct
the respondent autnoiiiies to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant to the
iepresentation dt.29-03-2023, pending disposal of the main Writ Petition, in the
interest of iustice.

Petition under Article 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one in
the mature of Writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more lhan Ac.
2400.00 gts., in Sy.No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar Mandal,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee W/o. aurhanuddin, and in
view of the land made into small plots to an extent of 100 Sq. Yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt. Haneefa Bee
viz., Md. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dl. O4-O4'2O23 followed
by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjust constitutional and violative of
Articles 21 and 300-4 of the Constitution of lndia and consequently direct the
respondent authorities to release the entire land and to dellver the vacant peaceful

and physical possession of the plots to the petitioners as per their memorandum of
Gift

Petition under Arlicle 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of Writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than Ac.
2400. 00 gts., in Sy. No= 7, 5itu31s6 at MansooraLrad Village, Saroomagar Mandat,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee W/o. Burhanuddin, and in
view of the land made into small plots to an extent of 100 Sq. yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt. Haneefa Bee
viz., Md. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
lr'lemorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dt. 31- 03- 2023 followed
by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjust unconstitutional and violative of
Articles 21 and 300- A of the Constitution of lndia and consequenfly direct the
respondent authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the vacant,
peaceful and physical possession of the plots to the petitioners as per their
Memorandum of Gift.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in suppo( of the petition, the High Court may be pleased to direct
the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant to the
representation dt. 31-03-2023, pending disposal of the main Writ petition, in the
interest of justice.

Petition under Article 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of Writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the enlire land admeasuring more than Ac.
2400.00 gts., in Sy.No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar Mandal,
Ranga Reciciy District, whieh was kept under the Custody of the Govei'nment as
care taker by the original paftedar Smt. Haneefa Bee W/o. Burhanuddin, and in
view of the land made into small plots to an extent of 100 Sq. Yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt. Haneefa Bee
viz., Md. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dt.O5-O4-2O23 followed
by personal visits, as being illegal, _arbitrary, unjust unconstitutional and violative of
Articles 2'1 and 300-4 of the Constitution of lndia and consequently diiect the
respondent authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the vacant,
neaceful and physical possession of the plots to the petitioners as per their
Ir/emorandum of Gift.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under Section '151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High Court may be pleased pleased
to direct the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps
pursuant to lhe representation dt. O5-O4-2O23, pending disposal of the main Writ
Petition, in the interest of justice.

Petition under Article 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumslances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of Writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than Ac.
2400.00 gts., in Sy.No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroomagar Mandal,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee W/o. Burhanuddin, and in
view of the land made into small plots to an extent of 100 Sq. Yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt. Haneefa Bee
viz., Md. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dl. 25-03-2023 followed
by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjust unconstitutional and violative of
Articles 21 and 300-A of the Constitution of lndia and consequently direct the
respondenl authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the vacant,

.zy'
peaceful and physical possession of the plots td the petitioners as per their
Memorandum of Gift.

Petition under Atlicle 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith' the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ' Order or Direction more parlicularly one in
the nature of Writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasering more than Ac'
24O0.OO gts., in Sy-No 7, situated at Mansoorabad Village' Saroornagar Mandal'
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar Smt' Haneefa Bee W/o' Burhanuddin' and in
view of the land made into small plots to an extent of 100 sq Yards to each
petitioner and allotted t6 the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt' Haneefa Bee
viz., Md. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt' Vasa m Tulsamma by way of
Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dt' 08-04-2023 followed
by personal visits, as being illegal' arbitrary, unjust unconstitulional and violative of
Articles 21 and 300-4 of the Constitution of lndia and consequently direct the
respondent authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the vacant'

q$
peaceful and physical possession of the plots to the petitioners as per lheir
Memorandum of Gift.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High Court may be pleased to direct
the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant to the
representation dl. O8lO4l2O23, pending disposal of the main Writ Petition, in the
interest of justice.

Petition under Arlicle 226 of the constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate wdt, order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the enlire land admeasuring more than Ac.
24oo.oo gts., in Sy.No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village' Saroornagar Mandal,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the custody of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar smt. Haneefa Bee wo. Burhanuddin, and in
view of the land made into smal plots to an extent of 100 sq. Yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt. Haneefa Bee
viz., Md. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dt. 08-04-2023 followed
by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjust unconstitutional and violative of
Articles 21 and 300-A of the constitution of lndia and consequently direct the
respondentauthoritiestoreleasetheentirelandandtodelivertheVacant,
peacefulandphysicalpossessionoftheplotstothepetitionersaSpertheir
Memorandum of Gift.

...RESPONDENTS
Petition under Anide 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of Writ of Nlandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than Ac.
2400.OO gts., in Sy.No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar lr4andel,
Range Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar Smt_ Haneefa Bee W/o. Burhanuddin, and in

VO
view of the land made into small plots to an extent of .100 Sq. yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of smt. Haneefa Bee
viz., Md. Yousuf Khan and his wife Sat Vasam Tulsamma by way of Memorandum
of Gift, inspite of the written representation dt. 2S-O3-2O23 followed by personal
visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjust unconstituti<inal and violative of Articles 21
and 300-4 of the Constitution of lndia and consequenfly direct the respondent
authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the vacant, peaceful and
physical possession of the plots to the petitioners as per their Memorandum of
Gift.
lA NO: I OF 2024
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High Court may be pleased to direct
lhe respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant to the
representation dt.25-03-2023, pending disposal of the main Writ petition, in the
interest of justice.
